arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-5- *  limited DMA windows. These functions utilize bounce buffers to
arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-6- *  copy data to/from buffers located outside the DMA region. This
arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-7- *  only works for systems in which DMA memory is at the bottom of
arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-8- *  RAM, the remainder of memory is at the top and the DMA memory
